Game History:
Round 1: Your guess: 25 / Result: too LOW

What is your guess this round? End your answer with: FINAL GUESS: [0-50]

[COMPLETION]
In this guessing game, we are using a binary search strategy to efficiently converge on the mystery number. The range for our guesses is between 0 and 50.

### Game History:
- **Round 1:** Our guess was 25, and the result was "too LOW." This means the mystery number is greater than 25.

### Updated Range:
- **New Range:** 26 to 50 (since 25 was too low, we adjust the lower bound to 26).

### Strategy:
To apply a binary search, we need to find the midpoint of the new range. The formula for the midpoint is:

\[
\text{Midpoint} = \left\lfloor \frac{\text{Lower Bound} + \text{Upper Bound}}{2} \right\rfloor
\]

### Calculation:
- **Lower Bound:** 26
- **Upper Bound:** 50

\[
\text{Midpoint} = \left\lfloor \frac{26 + 50}{2} \right\rfloor = \left\lfloor \frac{76}{2} \right\rfloor = 38
\]

Thus, the best guess for this round is 38.

FINAL GUESS: 38
